Conclusion: FIT数据集填补了虚拟试穿领域缺乏合身度数据的空白，为合身感知虚拟试穿设定了新的基准，并为未来研究提供了强大的数据集和基准测试平台。

Abstract: Given a person and a garment image, virtual try-on (VTO) aims to synthesize a realistic image of the person wearing the garment, while preserving their original pose and identity. Although recent VTO methods excel at visualizing garment appearance, they largely overlook a crucial aspect of the try-on experience: the accuracy of garment fit -- for example, depicting how an extra-large shirt looks on an extra-small person. A key obstacle is the absence of datasets that provide precise garment and body size information, particularly for "ill-fit" cases, where garments are significantly too large or too small. Consequently, current VTO methods default to generating well-fitted results regardless of the garment or person size.
  In this paper, we take the first steps towards solving this open problem. We introduce FIT (Fit-Inclusive Try-on), a large-scale VTO dataset comprising over 1.13M try-on image triplets accompanied by precise body and garment measurements. We overcome the challenges of data collection via a scalable synthetic strategy: (1) We programmatically generate 3D garments using GarmentCode and drape them via physics simulation to capture realistic garment fit. (2) We employ a novel re-texturing framework to transform synthetic renderings into photorealistic images while strictly preserving geometry. (3) We introduce person identity preservation into our re-texturing model to generate paired person images (same person, different garments) for supervised training. Finally, we leverage our FIT dataset to train a baseline fit-aware virtual try-on model. Our data and results set the new state-of-the-art for fit-aware virtual try-on, as well as offer a robust benchmark for future research. We will make all data and code publicly available on our project page: https://johannakarras.github.io/FIT.

</details>


### [146] [Self-Improving 4D Perception via Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08532)
*Nan Huang,Pengcheng Yu,Weijia Zeng,James M. Rehg,Angjoo Kanazawa,Haiwen Feng,Qianqian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SelfEvo：基于时空上下文不对称的自蒸馏框架，通过无标注视频持续改进预训练多视角重建模型，无需外部标注实现4D感知的自提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模多视角重建模型主要依赖完全监督训练，需要昂贵的3D/4D标注数据，特别是动态场景标注稀缺，限制了模型的可扩展性

Method: 提出自提升框架SelfEvo，引入基于时空上下文不对称的自蒸馏方案，系统研究损失信号、不对称形式等设计选择，使基于学习的4D感知无需外部标注即可自我改进

Result: 在8个基准测试中，SelfEvo持续改进预训练基线，在VGGT和π^3等基础模型上具有良好泛化性，动态场景提升显著：视频深度估计相对提升36.5%，相机估计提升20.1%

Conclusion: SelfEvo框架通过无标注视频实现多视角重建模型的自提升，有效解决动态场景标注稀缺问题，为4D感知的持续改进提供了有效途径

Abstract: Large-scale multi-view reconstruction models have made remarkable progress, but most existing approaches still rely on fully supervised training with ground-truth 3D/4D annotations. Such annotations are expensive and particularly scarce for dynamic scenes, limiting scalability. We propose SelfEvo, a self-improving framework that continually improves pretrained multi-view reconstruction models using unlabeled videos. SelfEvo introduces a self-distillation scheme using spatiotemporal context asymmetry, enabling self-improvement for learning-based 4D perception without external annotations. We systematically study design choices that make self-improvement effective, including loss signals, forms of asymmetry, and other training strategies. Across eight benchmarks spanning diverse datasets and domains, SelfEvo consistently improves pretrained baselines and generalizes across base models (e.g. VGGT and $π^3$), with significant gains on dynamic scenes. Overall, SelfEvo achieves up to 36.5% relative improvement in video depth estimation and 20.1% in camera estimation, without using any labeled data. Project Page: https://self-evo.github.io/.

</details>


### [147] [RewardFlow: Generate Images by Optimizing What You Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08536)
*Onkar Susladkar,Dong-Hwan Jang,Tushar Prakash,Adheesh Juvekar,Vedant Shah,Ayush Barik,Nabeel Bashir,Muntasir Wahed,Ritish Shrirao,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RewardFlow：通过多奖励朗之万动力学在推理时引导预训练扩散和流匹配模型的无反转框架，统一多种可微分奖励并引入基于VQA的细粒度语义监督奖励，通过提示感知自适应策略动态协调异质目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑和组合生成方法通常需要复杂的训练过程或反转操作，难以协调多个异质目标（语义对齐、感知保真度、局部接地、对象一致性、人类偏好）。需要一种无需训练或反转的推理时引导框架，能够统一协调多种奖励信号。

Method: 1. 提出RewardFlow框架，基于多奖励朗之万动力学在推理时引导预训练模型；2. 统一多种可微分奖励：语义对齐、感知保真度、局部接地、对象一致性、人类偏好；3. 引入基于VQA的可微分奖励，通过语言-视觉推理提供细粒度语义监督；4. 设计提示感知自适应策略：从指令中提取语义原语，推断编辑意图，在采样过程中动态调整奖励权重和步长。

Result: 在多个图像编辑和组合生成基准测试中，RewardFlow实现了最先进的编辑保真度和组合对齐性能。

Conclusion: RewardFlow提供了一种无需训练或反转的有效框架，通过多奖励朗之万动力学和自适应策略协调异质目标，显著提升了预训练扩散和流匹配模型在图像编辑和组合生成任务中的性能。

Abstract: We introduce RewardFlow, an inversion-free framework that steers pretrained diffusion and flow-matching models at inference time through multi-reward Langevin dynamics. RewardFlow unifies complementary differentiable rewards for semantic alignment, perceptual fidelity, localized grounding, object consistency, and human preference, and further introduces a differentiable VQA-based reward that provides fine-grained semantic supervision through language-vision reasoning. To coordinate these heterogeneous objectives, we design a prompt-aware adaptive policy that extracts semantic primitives from the instruction, infers edit intent, and dynamically modulates reward weights and step sizes throughout sampling. Across several image editing and compositional generation benchmarks, RewardFlow delivers state-of-the-art edit fidelity and compositional alignment.

</details>


### [148] [OpenVLThinkerV2: A Generalist Multimodal Reasoning Model for Multi-domain Visual Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08539)
*Wenbo Hu,Xin Chen,Yan Gao-Tian,Yihe Deng,Nanyun Peng,Kai-Wei Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了Gaussian GRPO (G²RPO)方法，通过将优势分布强制收敛到标准正态分布来解决多模态大语言模型强化学习中的奖励拓扑方差问题，并结合响应长度塑造和熵塑造机制，开发了OpenVLThinkerV2多模态通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO已成为多模态大语言模型强化学习的主流目标，但将其成功扩展到开源多模态通用模型面临两大挑战：1）不同视觉任务间奖励拓扑的极端方差；2）细粒度感知与多步推理能力平衡的固有困难。

Method: 提出Gaussian GRPO (G²RPO)，用非线性分布匹配替代标准线性缩放，强制任何任务的优势分布严格收敛到标准正态分布N(0,1)。在此基础上引入两个任务级塑造机制：1）响应长度塑造，动态激发复杂查询的扩展推理链，同时强制直接输出以增强视觉基础；2）熵塑造，紧密约束模型的探索区域，防止熵崩溃和熵爆炸。

Result: 基于G²RPO的增强训练稳定性，开发了OpenVLThinkerV2高度鲁棒的多模态通用模型。在18个多样化基准测试中表现出色，超越了强大的开源模型和领先的专有前沿模型。

Conclusion: G²RPO通过数学上强制优势分布收敛到标准正态分布，实现了任务间梯度公平性，减轻了对重尾异常值的脆弱性，并为正负奖励提供对称更新。结合任务级塑造机制，有效平衡了感知与推理能力，推动了开源多模态通用模型的发展。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has emerged as the de facto Reinforcement Learning (RL) objective driving recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models. However, extending this success to open-source multimodal generalist models remains heavily constrained by two primary challenges: the extreme variance in reward topologies across diverse visual tasks, and the inherent difficulty of balancing fine-grained perception with multi-step reasoning capabilities. To address these issues, we introduce Gaussian GRPO (G$^2$RPO), a novel RL training objective that replaces standard linear scaling with non-linear distributional matching. By mathematically forcing the advantage distribution of any given task to strictly converge to a standard normal distribution, $\mathcal{N}(0,1)$, G$^2$RPO theoretically ensures inter-task gradient equity, mitigates vulnerabilities to heavy-tail outliers, and offers symmetric update for positive and negative rewards. Leveraging the enhanced training stability provided by G$^2$RPO, we introduce two task-level shaping mechanisms to seamlessly balance perception and reasoning. First, response length shaping dynamically elicits extended reasoning chains for complex queries while enforce direct outputs to bolster visual grounding. Second, entropy shaping tightly bounds the model's exploration zone, effectively preventing both entropy collapse and entropy explosion. Integrating these methodologies, we present OpenVLThinkerV2, a highly robust, general-purpose multimodal model. Extensive evaluations across 18 diverse benchmarks demonstrate its superior performance over strong open-source and leading proprietary frontier models.

</details>


### [149] [AVGen-Bench: A Task-Driven Benchmark for Multi-Granular Evaluation of Text-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08540)
*Ziwei Zhou,Zeyuan Lai,Rui Wang,Yifan Yang,Zhen Xing,Yuqing Yang,Qi Dai,Lili Qiu,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVGen-Bench：首个面向真实场景的文本到音视频生成基准测试，通过多粒度评估框架揭示当前模型在语义可靠性方面的显著缺陷


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本到音视频生成评估存在碎片化问题，现有基准测试大多孤立评估音频和视频，或依赖粗糙的嵌入相似度，无法捕捉真实提示所需的细粒度联合正确性

Method: 提出AVGen-Bench基准测试，包含11个真实世界类别的高质量提示；设计多粒度评估框架，结合轻量级专家模型和多模态大语言模型，从感知质量到细粒度语义可控性进行全面评估

Result: 评估揭示了强烈的音视频美学质量与薄弱的语义可靠性之间的显著差距，包括文本渲染、语音连贯性、物理推理方面的持续失败，以及在音乐音高控制方面的普遍崩溃

Conclusion: AVGen-Bench为文本到音视频生成提供了全面的评估基准，揭示了当前模型在语义可靠性方面的关键缺陷，为未来研究指明了改进方向

Abstract: Text-to-Audio-Video (T2AV) generation is rapidly becoming a core interface for media creation, yet its evaluation remains fragmented. Existing benchmarks largely assess audio and video in isolation or rely on coarse embedding similarity, failing to capture the fine-grained joint correctness required by realistic prompts. We introduce AVGen-Bench, a task-driven benchmark for T2AV generation featuring high-quality prompts across 11 real-world categories. To support comprehensive assessment, we propose a multi-granular evaluation framework that combines lightweight specialist models with Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), enabling evaluation from perceptual quality to fine-grained semantic controllability. Our evaluation reveals a pronounced gap between strong audio-visual aesthetics and weak semantic reliability, including persistent failures in text rendering, speech coherence, physical reasoning, and a universal breakdown in musical pitch control. Code and benchmark resources are available at http://aka.ms/avgenbench.

</details>


### [150] [Seeing but Not Thinking: Routing Distraction in Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08541)
*Haolei Xu,Haiwen Hong,Hongxing Li,Rui Zhou,Yang Zhang,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Yongliang Shen,Weiming Lu,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现多模态MoE模型存在"看见但不思考"现象：模型能准确感知图像内容但在后续推理中失败，却能正确解决纯文本的相同问题。研究提出"路由分心"假设，并通过路由引导干预方法显著提升视觉推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态混合专家模型在视觉语言任务中表现出色，但研究者发现了一个令人困惑的现象：模型能准确感知图像内容却在后续推理中失败，而相同的纯文本问题却能正确解决。这揭示了当前多模态MoE模型在视觉输入处理中存在根本性缺陷，需要深入理解其工作机制。

Method: 首先系统分析验证跨模态语义共享的存在，排除语义对齐失败作为唯一解释。然后揭示视觉专家和领域专家在层级上的分离，发现图像输入在中间层（领域专家集中的层）引起显著的路由分歧。基于这些发现提出"路由分心"假设，并设计路由引导干预方法来增强领域专家激活。

Result: 在三个多模态MoE模型和六个基准测试上的实验显示了一致的改进，复杂视觉推理任务的性能提升高达3.17%。分析进一步表明，领域专家识别定位的是认知功能而非样本特定解决方案，使得方法能够有效跨任务迁移。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了多模态MoE模型中"看见但不思考"现象的根本原因在于路由机制在处理视觉输入时未能充分激活任务相关的推理专家。提出的路由引导干预方法通过增强领域专家激活有效解决了这一问题，为理解多模态模型的工作机制提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have achieved remarkable performance on vision-language tasks. However, we identify a puzzling phenomenon termed Seeing but Not Thinking: models accurately perceive image content yet fail in subsequent reasoning, while correctly solving identical problems presented as pure text. Through systematic analysis, we first verify that cross-modal semantic sharing exists in MoE architectures, ruling out semantic alignment failure as the sole explanation. We then reveal that visual experts and domain experts exhibit layer-wise separation, with image inputs inducing significant routing divergence from text inputs in middle layers where domain experts concentrate. Based on these findings, we propose the Routing Distraction hypothesis: when processing visual inputs, the routing mechanism fails to adequately activate task-relevant reasoning experts. To validate this hypothesis, we design a routing-guided intervention method that enhances domain expert activation. Experiments on three multimodal MoE models across six benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements, with gains of up to 3.17% on complex visual reasoning tasks. Our analysis further reveals that domain expert identification locates cognitive functions rather than sample-specific solutions, enabling effective transfer across tasks with different information structures.

</details>


### [151] [Act Wisely: Cultivating Meta-Cognitive Tool Use in Agentic Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08545)
*Shilin Yan,Jintao Tong,Hongwei Xue,Xiaojun Tang,Yangyang Wang,Kunyu Shi,Guannan Zhang,Ruixuan Li,Yixiong Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HDPO框架通过解耦精度和效率优化通道，解决多模态智能体盲目调用工具的问题，在保持任务准确性的同时大幅减少工具调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态智能体存在元认知缺陷，无法有效仲裁内部知识和外部工具的使用，导致盲目工具调用，造成延迟瓶颈和推理噪声。现有强化学习方法通过标量化奖励惩罚工具使用，但存在优化困境：惩罚过强会抑制必要工具使用，惩罚过弱则被精度奖励方差淹没。

Method: 提出HDPO框架，将工具效率从竞争性标量目标重构为严格条件性目标。通过避免奖励标量化，维护两个正交优化通道：精度通道最大化任务正确性，效率通道通过条件优势估计在准确轨迹中强制执行执行经济性。这种解耦架构自然诱导认知课程，迫使智能体先掌握任务解决，再精炼自给自足能力。

Result: 基于HDPO框架开发的Metis模型在广泛评估中，将工具调用次数减少数个数量级，同时提升推理准确性。

Conclusion: HDPO框架通过解耦精度和效率优化，有效解决了多模态智能体工具过度调用问题，在保持任务性能的同时显著提升执行效率，为智能体元认知能力的发展提供了新方向。

Abstract: The advent of agentic multimodal models has empowered systems to actively interact with external environments. However, current agents suffer from a profound meta-cognitive deficit: they struggle to arbitrate between leveraging internal knowledge and querying external utilities. Consequently, they frequently fall prey to blind tool invocation, resorting to reflexive tool execution even when queries are resolvable from the raw visual context. This pathological behavior precipitates severe latency bottlenecks and injects extraneous noise that derails sound reasoning. Existing reinforcement learning protocols attempt to mitigate this via a scalarized reward that penalizes tool usage. Yet, this coupled formulation creates an irreconcilable optimization dilemma: an aggressive penalty suppresses essential tool use, whereas a mild penalty is entirely subsumed by the variance of the accuracy reward during advantage normalization, rendering it impotent against tool overuse. To transcend this bottleneck, we propose HDPO, a framework that reframes tool efficiency from a competing scalar objective to a strictly conditional one. By eschewing reward scalarization, HDPO maintains two orthogonal optimization channels: an accuracy channel that maximizes task correctness, and an efficiency channel that enforces execution economy exclusively within accurate trajectories via conditional advantage estimation. This decoupled architecture naturally induces a cognitive curriculum-compelling the agent to first master task resolution before refining its self-reliance. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our resulting model, Metis, reduces tool invocations by orders of magnitude while simultaneously elevating reasoning accuracy.

</details>


### [152] [When Numbers Speak: Aligning Textual Numerals and Visual Instances in Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08546)
*Zhengyang Sun,Yu Chen,Xin Zhou,Xiaofan Li,Xiwu Chen,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NUMINA是一个无需训练的身份识别-引导框架，通过识别提示-布局不一致性并调制交叉注意力来改进文本到视频扩散模型中的计数准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到视频扩散模型在生成提示中指定数量的物体时存在困难，经常无法正确生成所需数量的对象，这限制了模型在需要精确计数的应用场景中的实用性。

Method: NUMINA采用训练免费的识别-引导框架：1）通过选择判别性的自注意力和交叉注意力头来识别提示-布局不一致性，推导可计数的潜在布局；2）保守地细化该布局；3）调制交叉注意力来引导重新生成。

Result: 在CountBench基准测试中，NUMINA将Wan2.1-1.3B模型的计数准确性提高了7.4%，在5B和14B模型上分别提高了4.9%和5.5%。同时改善了CLIP对齐性并保持了时间一致性。

Conclusion: 结构引导补充了种子搜索和提示增强，为实现计数准确的文本到视频扩散提供了一条实用路径。该方法无需训练，能有效改善扩散模型的数值对齐问题。

Abstract: Text-to-video diffusion models have enabled open-ended video synthesis, but often struggle with generating the correct number of objects specified in a prompt. We introduce NUMINA , a training-free identify-then-guide framework for improved numerical alignment. NUMINA identifies prompt-layout inconsistencies by selecting discriminative self- and cross-attention heads to derive a countable latent layout. It then refines this layout conservatively and modulates cross-attention to guide regeneration. On the introduced CountBench, NUMINA improves counting accuracy by up to 7.4% on Wan2.1-1.3B, and by 4.9% and 5.5% on 5B and 14B models, respectively. Furthermore, CLIP alignment is improved while maintaining temporal consistency. These results demonstrate that structural guidance complements seed search and prompt enhancement, offering a practical path toward count-accurate text-to-video diffusion. The code is available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/NUMINA.

</details>


### [153] [GaussiAnimate: Reconstruct and Rig Animatable Categories with Level of Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08547)
*Jiaxin Wang,Dongxin Lyu,Zeyu Cai,Zhiyang Dou,Cheng Lin,Anpei Chen,Yuliang Xiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Skelebones：一种将4D形状动态层级压缩为可控且富有表现力的骨架-皮肤绑定系统，通过自由形态骨骼、运动自适应骨架和部件运动匹配实现高质量角色重动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由形态骨骼能有效捕捉非刚性变形但缺乏直观控制的运动学结构，需要一种既能保持表达能力又具有可控性的骨骼绑定系统。

Method: 三步骤方法：1) 将时间一致的可变形高斯压缩为自由形态骨骼；2) 从规范高斯提取平均曲率骨架并进行时间细化；3) 通过非参数部件运动匹配绑定骨架和骨骼。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上验证，相比LBS提升17.3% PSNR，相比BoB提升21.7%，在低数据量（约1000帧）下部件运动匹配相比鲁棒LBS提升48.4% RMSE，优于GRU和MLP方法20%以上。

Conclusion: Skelebones系统成功将4D形状动态层级压缩为紧凑骨架，实现高质量角色重动画，在保持重建保真度的同时显著提升对新姿态的泛化能力。

Abstract: Free-form bones, that conform closely to the surface, can effectively capture non-rigid deformations, but lack a kinematic structure necessary for intuitive control. Thus, we propose a Scaffold-Skin Rigging System, termed "Skelebones", with three key steps: (1) Bones: compress temporally-consistent deformable Gaussians into free-form bones, approximating non-rigid surface deformations; (2) Skeleton: extract a Mean Curvature Skeleton from canonical Gaussians and refine it temporally, ensuring a category-agnostic, motion-adaptive, and topology-correct kinematic structure; (3) Binding: bind the skeleton and bones via non-parametric partwise motion matching (PartMM), synthesizing novel bone motions by matching, retrieving, and blending existing ones. Collectively, these three steps enable us to compress the Level of Dynamics of 4D shapes into compact skelebones that are both controllable and expressive. We validate our approach on both synthetic and real-world datasets, achieving significant improvements in reanimation performance across unseen poses-with 17.3% PSNR gains over Linear Blend Skinning (LBS) and 21.7% over Bag-of-Bones (BoB)-while maintaining excellent reconstruction fidelity, particularly for characters exhibiting complex non-rigid surface dynamics. Our Partwise Motion Matching algorithm demonstrates strong generalization to both Gaussian and mesh representations, especially under low-data regime (~1000 frames), achieving 48.4% RMSE improvement over robust LBS and outperforming GRU- and MLP-based learning methods by >20%. Code will be made publicly available for research purposes at cookmaker.cn/gaussianimate.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

### [154] [Label Leakage Attacks in Machine Unlearning: A Parameter and Inversion-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07386)
*Weidong Zheng,Kongyang Chen,Yao Huang,Yuanwei Guo,Yatie Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对类别遗忘场景，提出了四种攻击方法来推断目标模型中已遗忘的类别，包括基于模型参数的两种方法和基于模型反演的两种方法，并在多个数据集和遗忘算法上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能技术在面部识别等领域的广泛应用，数据隐私安全问题受到广泛关注，特别是隐私保护法律强调的"被遗忘权"。现有技术提出了各种遗忘方法，但这些方法可能无意中泄露已遗忘数据的类别信息。本文关注类别遗忘场景，分析在多种场景下已遗忘数据类别泄露的潜在问题。

Method: 提出了四种攻击方法：1）基于模型参数的判别特征构建（通过计算目标模型与辅助模型参数的点积或向量差），结合k-means聚类、Youden指数和决策树算法；2）基于模型反演的白盒攻击（梯度优化）和黑盒攻击（遗传算法）来重建类别原型样本，然后通过阈值准则和信息熵准则分析预测结果。

Result: 在四个标准数据集上针对五种最先进的遗忘算法进行了评估。实验结果表明，所提出的方法能够有效推断目标模型遗忘的类别，并对每种方法的优势和局限性进行了详细分析。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了现有遗忘方法在类别隐私保护方面的潜在漏洞，提出了多种攻击方法来检测类别泄露问题，为设计更安全的遗忘算法提供了重要参考。

Abstract: With the widespread application of artificial intelligence technologies in face recognition and other fields, data privacy security issues have received extensive attention, especially the \textit{right to be forgotten} emphasized by numerous privacy protection laws. Existing technologies have proposed various unlearning methods, but they may inadvertently leak the categories of unlearned data. This paper focuses on the category unlearning scenario, analyzes the potential problems of category leakage of unlearned data in multiple scenarios, and proposes four attack methods from the perspectives of model parameters and model inversion based on attackers with different knowledge backgrounds. At the level of model parameters, we construct discriminative features by computing either dot products or vector differences between the parameters of the target model and those of auxiliary models trained on subsets of retained data and unrelated data, respectively. These features are then processed via k-means clustering, Youden's Index, and decision tree algorithms to achieve accurate identification of the forgotten class. In the model inversion domain, we design a gradient optimization-based white-box attack and a genetic algorithm-based black-box attack to reconstruct class-prototypical samples. The prediction profiles of these synthesized samples are subsequently analyzed using a threshold criterion and an information entropy criterion to infer the forgotten class. We evaluate the proposed attacks on four standard datasets against five state-of-the-art unlearning algorithms, providing a detailed analysis of the strengths and limitations of each method. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach can effectively infer the classes forgotten by the target model.

</details>


### [155] [Private Seeds, Public LLMs: Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07486)
*Qian Ma,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RPSG是一种利用差分隐私和私有种子生成真实且保护隐私的合成文本数据的方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在合成数据生成方面表现出强大能力，但生成包含个人信息的私有文本时需要在隐私保护和数据实用性之间取得平衡

Method: 提出RPSG方法，结合形式化差分隐私机制和私有种子（包含个人信息的文本），生成真实且保护隐私的合成数据

Result: 与最先进的私有合成数据生成方法相比，RPSG在保持对私有数据高保真度的同时提供了强大的隐私保护

Conclusion: RPSG方法能够有效平衡合成数据的真实性和隐私保护需求，为私有文本数据的合成生成提供了可行的解决方案

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for synthetic data generation. A particularly important use case is producing synthetic replicas of private text, which requires carefully balancing privacy and utility. We propose Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation (RPSG), which leverages privacy-preserving mechanisms, including formal differential privacy (DP); and private seeds, in particular text containing personal information, to generate realistic synthetic data. Comprehensive experiments against state-of-the-art private synthetic data generation methods demonstrate that RPSG achieves high fidelity to private data while providing strong privacy protection.

</details>


### [156] [TRUSTDESC: Preventing Tool Poisoning in LLM Applications via Trusted Description Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07536)
*Hengkai Ye,Zhechang Zhang,Jinyuan Jia,Hong Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRUSTDESC是一个防止工具中毒攻击的框架，通过从工具实现自动生成可信描述来对抗显式和隐式TPA攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型依赖外部工具执行任务，但工具集成引入了新的提示注入攻击面：工具中毒攻击。现有防御主要检测异常指令，对隐式TPA无效，需要从根本上防止工具描述被篡改。

Method: TRUSTDESC采用三阶段流水线：1) SliceMin通过可达性感知静态分析和LLM引导的去冗余提取最小工具相关代码片段；2) DescGen从代码片段合成描述，缓解误导或对抗性代码伪影；3) DynVer通过执行合成任务和验证行为声明进行动态验证来精炼描述。

Result: 在52个跨多个工具生态系统的真实世界工具上评估，TRUSTDESC生成准确工具描述，提高任务完成率，从根本上缓解隐式TPA，时间和金钱开销最小。

Conclusion: TRUSTDESC是首个通过从实现自动生成可信描述来防止工具中毒的框架，有效对抗显式和隐式TPA攻击，为LLM工具集成提供安全保障。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to perform time-sensitive tasks and real-world actions. While tool integration expands LLM capabilities, it also introduces a new prompt-injection attack surface: tool poisoning attacks (TPAs). Attackers manipulate tool descriptions by embedding malicious instructions (explicit TPAs) or misleading claims (implicit TPAs) to influence model behavior and tool selection. Existing defenses mainly detect anomalous instructions and remain ineffective against implicit TPAs. In this paper, we present TRUSTDESC, the first framework for preventing tool poisoning by automatically generating trusted tool descriptions from implementations. TRUSTDESC derives implementation-faithful descriptions through a three-stage pipeline. SliceMin performs reachability-aware static analysis and LLM-guided debloating to extract minimal tool-relevant code slices. DescGen synthesizes descriptions from these slices while mitigating misleading or adversarial code artifacts. DynVer refines descriptions through dynamic verification by executing synthesized tasks and validating behavioral claims. We evaluate TRUSTDESC on 52 real-world tools across multiple tool ecosystems. Results show that TRUSTDESC produces accurate tool descriptions that improve task completion rates while mitigating implicit TPAs at their root, with minimal time and monetary overhead.

</details>


### [157] [MCP-DPT: A Defense-Placement Taxonomy and Coverage Analysis for Model Context Protocol Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07551)
*Mehrdad Rostamzadeh,Sidhant Narula,Nahom Birhan,Mohammad Ghasemigol,Daniel Takabi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MCP（模型上下文协议）的防御导向安全分析框架，通过分层分类法组织攻击并确定防御责任点，发现当前防御主要集中在工具层，而在主机编排、传输和供应链层存在安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP使LLM能够动态发现和调用第三方工具，显著扩展了智能体能力，但引入了独特的安全格局。现有研究主要是攻击导向或基准测试驱动，缺乏对MCP多参与者架构中防御责任归属的指导，这在实际部署中存在问题。

Method: 提出了防御导向的MCP安全分析方法，引入分层对齐的分类法，将攻击按负责执行的架构组件进行组织。威胁被映射到六个MCP层，识别主要和次要防御点，支持在对手控制工具、服务器或生态系统组件情况下的深度防御推理。

Result: 将现有学术和行业防御措施映射到该框架后，发现保护措施不均衡且主要集中在工具层，而在主机编排、传输和供应链层存在持续的安全缺口。这表明许多MCP安全弱点源于架构错位而非孤立的实现缺陷。

Conclusion: MCP的安全问题主要源于其多参与者架构和分布式信任边界，需要系统性的防御责任分配。通过分层分类法可以更有效地识别防御缺口，推动更均衡的安全保护措施部署，特别是在当前薄弱的架构层。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables large language models (LLMs) to dynamically discover and invoke third-party tools, significantly expanding agent capabilities while introducing a distinct security landscape. Unlike prompt-only interactions, MCP exposes pre-execution artifacts, shared context, multi-turn workflows, and third-party supply chains to adversarial influence across independently operated components. While recent work has identified MCP-specific attacks and evaluated defenses, existing studies are largely attack-centric or benchmark-driven, providing limited guidance on where mitigation responsibility should reside within the MCP architecture. This is problematic given MCP's multi-party design and distributed trust boundaries. We present a defense-placement-oriented security analysis of MCP, introducing a layer-aligned taxonomy that organizes attacks by the architectural component responsible for enforcement. Threats are mapped across six MCP layers, and primary and secondary defense points are identified to support principled defense-in-depth reasoning under adversaries controlling tools, servers, or ecosystem components. A structured mapping of existing academic and industry defenses onto this framework reveals uneven and predominantly tool-centric protection, with persistent gaps at the host orchestration, transport, and supply-chain layers. These findings suggest that many MCP security weaknesses stem from architectural misalignment rather than isolated implementation flaws.

</details>


### [158] [MEV-ACE: Identity-Authenticated Fair Ordering for Proposer-Controlled MEV Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07568)
*Jian Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MEV ACE是一个公平排序协议，通过注册经济身份、认证承诺机制和可验证延迟随机性，消除区块生产者对交易排序的单方面控制，解决MEV中的不公平问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV（最大可提取价值）对区块链公平性构成结构性威胁，因为区块生产者可以观察待处理交易并单方面决定其排序或包含。现有缓解措施存在两个未解决的缺陷：承诺未通过可惩罚身份进行认证，包含义务缺乏其他验证者可验证的可转移证据。

Method: MEV ACE结合三种机制：1) 使用从ACE GF框架确定性派生并在链上质押的注册经济身份；2) 使用带验证者接收阈值的认证承诺和开放消息，使可接纳性和包含义务可独立审计；3) 使用基于可验证延迟的随机性，在可接纳承诺集固定后才确定交易顺序。

Result: 在标准假设下，协议具有三个特性：可接纳集锁定后的顺序不可预测性、签名不可伪造性下的承诺真实性，以及获得阈值承诺和开放收据的交易的可问责包含性。当生产者和用户质押超过单时隙非法执行或选择性不开放的收益时，MEV ACE消除了区块生产者对抢先交易、三明治攻击和已接纳交易审查的单方面自由裁量权。

Conclusion: MEV ACE协议在结构上保持单时隙，无需阈值解密委员会，兼容ML-DSA 44等后量子签名方案，有效解决了MEV中的公平排序问题，消除了区块生产者的单方面控制权。

Abstract: Maximal Extractable Value, or MEV, remains a structural threat to blockchain fairness because a block producer can often observe pending transactions and unilaterally decide their ordering or inclusion. Existing mitigations hide transaction contents or outsource ordering, but they often leave two gaps unresolved. First, commitments are not authenticated by slashable identities. Second, inclusion obligations are not backed by transferable evidence that other validators can verify. This paper presents MEV ACE, a fair ordering protocol for proposer controlled ordering MEV. MEV ACE combines three mechanisms. First, it uses registered economic identities whose authentication keys are deterministically derived from the ACE GF framework and bonded on chain. Second, it uses authenticated commit and open messages with validator receipt thresholds, which make admissibility and inclusion obligations independently auditable. Third, it uses verifiable delay based randomness to determine transaction order only after the admissible commitment set is fixed. We formalize the protocol in a Byzantine fault tolerant validator model with threshold receipts and show three properties under standard assumptions: order unpredictability after the admissible set is locked, commitment authenticity under signature unforgeability, and accountable inclusion for transactions that obtain threshold commit and open receipts. Under these conditions, and when producer and user bonds exceed the one slot gain from invalid execution or selective non opening, MEV ACE removes unilateral proposer discretion over front running, sandwich attacks, and censorship against admitted transactions. The protocol remains single slot in structure, requires no threshold decryption committee, and is compatible with post quantum signature schemes such as ML DSA 44.

</details>


### [159] [Interpreting the Error of Differentially Private Median Queries through Randomization Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07581)
*Thomas Humphries,Tim Li,Shufan Zhang,Karl Knopf,Xi He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PostRI方法，在差分隐私中位数估计后计算随机化区间，相比现有方法提升14%-850%的效用，同时保持窄区间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私统计中，由于添加了噪声，实践者难以解释统计结果质量。现有方法（如随机化区间RI）在噪声分布依赖于输入（如中位数）时，会降低中位数本身的质量来获得高质量RI

Method: 提出PostRI方法，先估计中位数，然后计算随机化区间。该方法允许在中位数估计完成后计算RI，避免为获得高质量RI而降低中位数估计质量

Result: PostRI方法相比相关工作，中位数估计效用提升14%-850%，同时保持窄的随机化区间

Conclusion: PostRI方法解决了差分隐私中位数估计中效用与解释性权衡问题，实现了高质量中位数估计与窄随机化区间的双重目标

Abstract: It can be difficult for practitioners to interpret the quality of differentially private (DP) statistics due to the added noise. One method to help analysts understand the amount of error introduced by DP is to return a Randomization Interval (RI), along with the statistic. A RI is a type of confidence interval that bounds the error introduced by DP. For queries where the noise distribution depends on the input, such as the median, prior work degrades the quality of the median itself to obtain a high-quality RI. In this work, we propose PostRI, a solution to compute a RI after the median has been estimated. PostRI enables a median estimation with 14%-850% higher utility than related work, while maintaining a narrow RI.

</details>


### [160] [TrajGuard: Streaming Hidden-state Trajectory Detection for Decoding-time Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07727)
*Cheng Liu,Xiaolei Liu,Xingyu Li,Bangzhou Xin,Kangyi Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrajGuard：基于解码时隐藏状态轨迹的实时越狱防御框架，无需训练，通过滑动窗口聚合风险信号实现95%平均防御率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱防御主要依赖静态检测（提示、输出或内部状态），忽略了解码过程中风险的动态演变，导致解码轨迹中的风险信号未被充分利用，构成当前防御系统的关键盲点

Method: 提出TrajGuard框架，基于观察发现解码阶段关键层的隐藏状态比输入越狱提示携带更强、更稳定的风险信号。通过滑动窗口聚合隐藏状态轨迹来实时量化风险，仅当局部窗口内风险持续超过阈值时触发轻量级语义裁决，从而立即中断或约束后续解码

Result: 在12种越狱攻击和多种开源LLM上的广泛实验显示，TrajGuard实现平均95%的防御率，检测延迟降至5.2毫秒/令牌，误报率低于1.5%

Conclusion: 解码过程中的隐藏状态轨迹能有效支持实时越狱检测，为无需模型修改的防御提供了有前景的方向，证明了动态解码时监控的重要性

Abstract: Existing jailbreak defense paradigms primarily rely on static detection of prompts, outputs, or internal states, often neglecting the dynamic evolution of risk during decoding. This oversight leaves risk signals embedded in decoding trajectories underutilized, constituting a critical blind spot in current defense systems. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that hidden states in critical layers during the decoding phase carry stronger and more stable risk signals than input jailbreak prompts. Specifically, the hidden representations of tokens generated during jailbreak attempts progressively approach high-risk regions in the latent space. Based on this observation, we propose TrajGuard, a training-free, decoding-time defense framework. TrajGuard aggregates hidden-state trajectories via a sliding window to quantify risk in real time, triggering a lightweight semantic adjudication only when risk within a local window persistently exceeds a threshold. This mechanism enables the immediate interruption or constraint of subsequent decoding. Extensive experiments across 12 jailbreak attacks and various open-source LLMs show that TrajGuard achieves an average defense rate of 95%. Furthermore, it reduces detection latency to 5.2 ms/token while maintaining a false positive rate below 1.5%. These results confirm that hidden-state trajectories during decoding can effectively support real-time jailbreak detection, highlighting a promising direction for defenses without model modification.

</details>


### [161] [The Art of (Mis)alignment: How Fine-Tuning Methods Effectively Misalign and Realign LLMs in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07754)
*Rui Zhang,Hongwei Li,Yun Shen,Xinyue Shen,Wenbo Jiang,Guowen Xu,Yang Liu,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大型语言模型对齐与错位问题，发现攻击与防御机制存在不对称性：ORPO在错位攻击中最有效，而DPO在重新对齐防御中表现最佳但会牺牲模型实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型部署存在伦理和安全风险，攻击者可能利用对齐技术破坏模型安全性（错位），导致错位模型在开放平台传播放大危害。因此需要对第三方模型进行重新对齐以确保安全部署。

Method: 评估了四种监督微调（SFT）和两种偏好微调（PFT）方法在四个流行安全对齐LLM上的效果，研究错位攻击、重新对齐防御及其相互作用的机制。

Result: 发现攻击与防御机制不对称：ORPO在错位攻击中最有效，DPO在重新对齐防御中表现最佳但会牺牲模型实用性；识别了模型特定抵抗性、多轮对抗动态的残留效应等发现。

Conclusion: 需要强大的安全防护措施和定制化的安全对齐策略来减轻LLM部署中的潜在风险，攻击与防御的不对称性要求针对性的安全策略。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) raises significant ethical and safety concerns. While LLM alignment techniques are adopted to improve model safety and trustworthiness, adversaries can exploit these techniques to undermine safety for malicious purposes, resulting in \emph{misalignment}. Misaligned LLMs may be published on open platforms to magnify harm. To address this, additional safety alignment, referred to as \emph{realignment}, is necessary before deploying untrusted third-party LLMs. This study explores the efficacy of fine-tuning methods in terms of misalignment, realignment, and the effects of their interplay. By evaluating four Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and two Preference Fine-Tuning (PFT) methods across four popular safety-aligned LLMs, we reveal a mechanism asymmetry between attack and defense. While Odds Ratio Preference Optimization (ORPO) is most effective for misalignment, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) excels in realignment, albeit at the expense of model utility. Additionally, we identify model-specific resistance, residual effects of multi-round adversarial dynamics, and other noteworthy findings. These findings highlight the need for robust safeguards and customized safety alignment strategies to mitigate potential risks in the deployment of LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangrui4041/The-Art-of-Mis-alignment.

</details>


### [162] [Anamorphic Encryption with CCA Security: A Standard Model Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07771)
*Shujun Wang,Jianting Ning,Qinyi Li,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个在标准模型下实现强IND-CCA安全的通用变形密钥封装机制(AKEM)，解决了现有变形加密方案仅依赖CPA安全性的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变形加密研究主要依赖CPA安全性，在标准模型下构建通用的CCA安全变形方案一直是一个未解决的开放性问题。特别是在接收者变形场景中，用户需要在被迫交出密钥时仍能保护隐藏信息，这需要更强的安全保证。

Method: 形式化定义了变形密钥封装机制(AKEM)，包括公钥(PKAKEM)和对称密钥(SKAKEM)变体。提出了通用构造方法，可使用任何支持随机数恢复的KEM进行实例化。通过将变形特性集成到KEM-DEM范式中，利用基础KEM的单射性质确保密文与随机数之间的唯一映射。

Result: 实现了强IND-CCA(sIND-CCA)安全性的隐蔽信道，在标准模型中提供了严格的形式化证明，能够抵抗控制解封装密钥的"独裁者"攻击。该框架显著增强了隐蔽信道在现代密码基础设施中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了变形加密领域的重要空白，首次在标准模型下实现了通用的CCA安全变形方案。通过形式化AKEM原语并提供通用构造，为隐蔽通信提供了更强的安全保障，特别是在后妥协场景中保护隐藏信息的能力得到显著提升。

Abstract: Anamorphic encryption serves as a vital tool for covert communication, maintaining secrecy even during post-compromise scenarios. Particularly in the receiver-anamorphic setting, a user can shield hidden messages even when coerced into surrendering their secret keys. However, a major bottleneck in existing research is the reliance on CPA-security, leaving the construction of a generic, CCA-secure anamorphic scheme in the standard model as a persistent open challenge. To bridge this gap, we formalize the Anamorphic Key Encapsulation Mechanism (AKEM), encompassing both Public-Key (PKAKEM) and Symmetric-Key (SKAKEM) variants. We propose generic constructions for these primitives, which can be instantiated using any KEM that facilitates randomness recovery. Notably, our framework achieves strong IND-CCA (sIND-CCA) security for the covert channel. We provide a rigorous formal proof in the standard model, demonstrating resilience against a "dictator" who controls the decapsulation key. The security of our approach is anchored in the injective property of the base KEM, which ensures a unique mapping between ciphertexts and randomness. By integrating anamorphism into the KEM-DEM paradigm, our work significantly enhances the practical utility of covert channels within modern cryptographic infrastructures.

</details>


### [163] [BRASP: Boolean Range Queries over Encrypted Spatial Data with Access and Search Pattern Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07797)
*Jing Zhang,Ganxuan Yang,Yifei Yang,Siqi Wen,Zhengyang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BRASP是一种支持加密空间数据上布尔范围查询的可搜索加密方案，通过希尔伯特曲线编码和双服务器架构隐藏搜索模式和访问模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可搜索加密方案主要针对关键词查询，且大多泄露搜索模式和访问模式，无法有效支持加密空间数据的布尔范围查询，存在隐私泄露风险

Method: 结合希尔伯特曲线前缀编码与加密的前缀-ID和关键词-ID倒排索引，支持空间范围过滤和合取关键词匹配；采用双服务器架构，通过索引洗牌和ID字段重分布隐藏模式

Result: 实验评估表明BRASP能有效保护查询隐私，同时保持相对较低的计算和通信开销；支持动态更新并实现前向安全性

Conclusion: BRASP为加密空间数据的布尔范围查询提供了隐私保护解决方案，通过双服务器架构和索引洗牌技术有效隐藏搜索和访问模式

Abstract: Searchable Encryption (SE) enables users to query outsourced encrypted data while preserving data confidentiality. However, most efficient schemes still leak the search pattern and access pattern, which may allow an honest-but-curious cloud server to infer query contents, user interests, or returned records from repeated searches and observed results. Existing pattern-hiding solutions mainly target keyword queries and do not naturally support Boolean range queries over encrypted spatial data. This paper presents BRASP, a searchable encryption scheme for Boolean range queries over encrypted spatial data. BRASP combines Hilbert-curve-based prefix encoding with encrypted prefix--ID and keyword--ID inverted indexes to support efficient spatial range filtering and conjunctive keyword matching. To hide the search pattern and access pattern under a dual-server setting, BRASP integrates index shuffling for encrypted keyword and prefix entries with ID-field redistribution across two non-colluding cloud servers. BRASP also supports dynamic updates and achieves forward security. We formalize the security of BRASP through confidentiality, shuffle indistinguishability, query unforgeability, and forward-security analyses, and we evaluate its performance experimentally on a real-world dataset. The results show that BRASP effectively protects query privacy while incurring relatively low computation and communication overhead. To facilitate reproducibility and further research, the source code of BRASP is publicly available at https://github.com/Egbert-Lannister/BRASP

</details>


### [164] [A Hardware-Anchored Privacy Middleware for PII Sharing Across Heterogeneous Embedded Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07839)
*Aditya Sabbineni,Pravin Nagare,Devendra Dahiphale,Preetam Dedu,Willison Lopes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: UDSS是一个平台无关的框架，用于在消费电子设备上实现安全、隐私优先的个人身份信息交换，通过上下文范围执行机制区分登录和注册流程，减少用户配置延迟65%并降低PII过度暴露风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和智能家居生态系统的快速扩张导致用户数据管理在智能电视、游戏机、机顶盒等消费电子设备上呈现碎片化。当前这些设备上的用户配置过程存在高摩擦，需要手动数据输入且数据共享实践不透明。

Method: 提出用户数据共享系统（UDSS），这是一个平台无关的框架，采用上下文范围执行（CSE）机制，根据用户意图（区分登录和注册工作流）以编程方式限制数据暴露。与云锚定的身份标准（如FIDO2/WebAuthn）不同，UDSS专为共享、设备中心的消费电子环境设计，其中不能假设持久的用户-设备绑定。进一步提出分层访问模型，平衡开发者需求与法规合规性（GDPR/CCPA）。在参考的ARMv8 Linux中间件上实现了概念验证。

Result: 概念验证实施表明，UDSS将用户配置延迟减少了65%，并通过协议强制执行的数据最小化显著降低了PII过度暴露风险。该框架为异构消费电子市场提供了标准化的身份管理方法。

Conclusion: UDSS为消费电子设备上的安全、隐私优先的PII交换提供了一个有效的标准化框架，特别适用于共享设备环境，在减少用户摩擦的同时增强了数据隐私保护。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and smart home ecosystems has led to a fragmented landscape of user data management across consumer electronics (CE) such as Smart TVs, gaming consoles, and set-top boxes. Current onboarding processes on these devices are characterized by high friction due to manual data entry and opaque data-sharing practices. This paper introduces the User Data Sharing System (UDSS), a platform-agnostic framework designed to facilitate secure, privacy-first PII (Personally Identifiable Information) exchange between device platforms and third-party applications. Our system implements a Contextual Scope Enforcement (CSE) mechanism that programmatically restricts data exposure based on user intent - specifically distinguishing between Sign-In and Sign-Up workflows. Unlike cloud-anchored identity standards such as FIDO2/WebAuthn, UDSS is designed for shared, device-centric CE environments where persistent user-to-device binding cannot be assumed. We further propose a tiered access model that balances developer needs with regulatory compliance (GDPR/CCPA). A proof-of-concept implementation on a reference ARMv8 Linux-based middleware demonstrates that UDSS reduces user onboarding latency by 65% and measurably reduces PII over-exposure risk through protocol-enforced data minimization. This framework provides a standardized approach to identity management in the heterogeneous CE market.

</details>


### [165] [xDup: Privacy-Preserving Deduplication for Humanitarian Organizations using Fuzzy PSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08019)
*Tim Rausch,Sylvain Chatel,Wouter Lueks*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: xDup：一种用于人道主义援助的隐私保护跨组织去重系统，比现有方案快两个数量级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义组织在危机情况下帮助确保人们生计，通常多个组织在同一地区运作。为确保有限预算能帮助尽可能多的人，组织需要进行跨组织去重以检测重复注册并确保受助者最多只从一个组织获得援助。当前去重方法存在隐私风险，可能将脆弱援助接收者的数据与其他组织共享。

Method: 提出xDup系统，基于Fuzzy PSI（模糊私有集合交集）技术，并开发了otFPSI协议——一种在汉明空间中无需输入假设的具体高效Fuzzy PSI协议。该系统满足人道主义组织的实际需求。

Result: xDup比当前解决方案快两个数量级，otFPSI协议比现有Fuzzy PSI协议更高效。系统满足人道主义组织的实际需求，适合真实人道主义任务。

Conclusion: xDup是一种实用、隐私友好的跨组织去重系统，能有效解决人道主义援助中的重复注册问题，同时保护脆弱受助者的隐私，且性能显著优于现有方案。

Abstract: Humanitarian organizations help to ensure people's livelihoods in crisis situations. Typically, multiple organizations operate in the same region. To ensure that the limited budget of these organizations can help as many people as possible, organizations perform cross-organizational deduplication to detect duplicate registrations and ensure recipients receive aid from at most one organization. Current deduplication approaches risk privacy harm to vulnerable aid recipients by sharing their data with other organizations. We analyzed the needs of humanitarian organizations to identify the requirements for privacy-friendly cross-organizational deduplication fit for real-life humanitarian missions. We present xDup, a new practical deduplication system that meets the requirements of humanitarian organizations and is two orders of magnitude faster than current solutions. xDup builds on Fuzzy PSI, and we present otFPSI, a concretely efficient Fuzzy PSI protocol for Hamming Space without input assumptions. We show that it is more efficient than existing Fuzzy PSI protocols.

</details>


### [166] [PrivFedTalk: Privacy-Aware Federated Diffusion with Identity-Stable Adapters for Personalized Talking-Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08037)
*Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PrivFedTalk是一个隐私感知的联邦学习框架，用于个性化说话头像生成，结合条件潜在扩散模型和参数高效的身份适配器，避免原始数据共享，保护用户隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统说话头像生成训练依赖集中的面部视频和语音数据集，存在严重的隐私问题。个性化说话头像生成中，身份特定数据高度敏感，通常无法跨用户或设备共享，需要隐私保护的解决方案。

Method: 1. 结合条件潜在扩散模型与参数高效的身份适配器（LoRA）；2. 共享扩散主干在客户端间训练，每个客户端从本地私有音视频数据学习轻量级身份适配器；3. 身份稳定联邦聚合（ISFA）使用隐私安全的标量可靠性信号加权客户端更新；4. 时间去噪一致性（TDC）正则化减少帧间漂移、闪烁和身份漂移；5. 应用安全聚合和客户端级差分隐私保护更新侧隐私。

Result: 在多种训练和聚合条件下与FedAvg和FedProx进行比较实验，显示稳定的联邦优化和成功的端到端训练与评估。支持在受限资源下进行隐私感知的个性化说话头像训练。

Conclusion: PrivFedTalk证明了在联邦环境中进行隐私感知个性化说话头像训练的可行性，但需要进一步标准化评估来验证更强的组件级、隐私-效用权衡和定性声明。

Abstract: Talking-head generation has advanced rapidly with diffusion-based generative models, but training usually depends on centralized face-video and speech datasets, raising major privacy concerns. The problem is more acute for personalized talking-head generation, where identity-specific data are highly sensitive and often cannot be pooled across users or devices. PrivFedTalk is presented as a privacy-aware federated framework for personalized talking-head generation that combines conditional latent diffusion with parameter-efficient identity adaptation. A shared diffusion backbone is trained across clients, while each client learns lightweight LoRA identity adapters from local private audio-visual data, avoiding raw data sharing and reducing communication cost. To address heterogeneous client distributions, Identity-Stable Federated Aggregation (ISFA) weights client updates using privacy-safe scalar reliability signals computed from on-device identity consistency and temporal stability estimates. Temporal-Denoising Consistency (TDC) regularization is introduced to reduce inter-frame drift, flicker, and identity drift during federated denoising. To limit update-side privacy risk, secure aggregation and client-level differential privacy are applied to adapter updates. The implementation supports both low-memory GPU execution and multi-GPU client-parallel training on heterogeneous shared hardware. Comparative experiments on the present setup across multiple training and aggregation conditions with PrivFedTalk, FedAvg, and FedProx show stable federated optimization and successful end-to-end training and evaluation under constrained resources. The results support the feasibility of privacy-aware personalized talking-head training in federated environments, while suggesting that stronger component-wise, privacy-utility, and qualitative claims need further standardized evaluation.

</details>


### [167] [TADP-RME: A Trust-Adaptive Differential Privacy Framework for Enhancing Reliability of Data-Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08113)
*Labani Halder,Payel Sadhukhan,Sarbani Palit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TADP-RME：一种信任自适应差分隐私框架，通过逆信任分数动态调整隐私预算，并使用反向流形嵌入破坏局部几何结构，提升对抗环境下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方案使用固定隐私预算，导致在用户信任异质情况下无法灵活权衡效用与隐私。同时，仅添加噪声的差分隐私保留了数据几何结构，易被推理攻击利用造成隐私泄露。

Method: 提出TADP-RME框架：1）引入[0,1]范围的逆信任分数，自适应调节隐私预算，实现效用与隐私的平滑过渡；2）使用反向流形嵌入进行非线性变换，破坏局部几何关系，同时通过后处理保持形式化差分隐私保证。

Result: 理论分析和实验结果表明，该方法改善了隐私-效用权衡，将攻击成功率降低高达3.1%且无明显效用损失。在对抗推理攻击方面持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TADP-RME为对抗环境中的可靠学习提供了统一框架，通过信任自适应隐私预算调节和几何结构破坏，有效增强了系统在异质用户信任下的可靠性。

Abstract: Ensuring reliability in adversarial settings necessitates treating privacy as a foundational component of data-driven systems. While differential privacy and cryptographic protocols offer strong guarantees, existing schemes rely on a fixed privacy budget, leading to a rigid utility-privacy trade-off that fails under heterogeneous user trust. Moreover, noise-only differential privacy preserves geometric structure, which inference attacks exploit, causing privacy leakage.
  We propose TADP-RME (Trust-Adaptive Differential Privacy with Reverse Manifold Embedding), a framework that enhances reliability under varying levels of user trust. It introduces an inverse trust score in the range [0,1] to adaptively modulate the privacy budget, enabling smooth transitions between utility and privacy. Additionally, Reverse Manifold Embedding applies a nonlinear transformation to disrupt local geometric relationships while preserving formal differential privacy guarantees through post-processing.
  Theoretical and empirical results demonstrate improved privacy-utility trade-offs, reducing attack success rates by up to 3.1 percent without significant utility degradation. The framework consistently outperforms existing methods against inference attacks, providing a unified approach for reliable learning in adversarial environments.

</details>


### [168] [Towards Identification and Intervention of Safety-Critical Parameters in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08297)
*Weiwei Qi,Zefeng Wu,Tianhang Zheng,Zikang Zhang,Xiaojun Jia,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ESI框架量化LLM安全机制，发现不同架构的安全关键参数分布模式，并基于此开发SET和SPA两种针对性安全干预方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对LLM安全机制的清晰理解，阻碍了开发精确可靠的安全干预方法，需要更好的理解和控制LLM安全性

Method: 提出Expected Safety Impact (ESI)框架量化参数对安全的影响；基于ESI分析发现密集LLM和MoE模型的安全关键参数分布模式；开发Safety Enhancement Tuning (SET)和Safety Preserving Adaptation (SPA)两种干预范式

Result: SET仅更新1%参数100次迭代可将未对齐LLM攻击成功率降低超50%；SPA在1000次指令微调后能将安全性能下降控制在1%以内；发现密集LLM安全关键参数主要位于中间层的V矩阵和MLP，MoE模型则集中在后层MLP

Conclusion: ESI框架为理解和控制LLM安全机制提供了系统方法，SET和SPA分别实现了高效的安全增强和保持，为LLM安全干预提供了精确可靠的技术路径

Abstract: Ensuring Large Language Model (LLM) safety is crucial, yet the lack of a clear understanding about safety mechanisms hinders the development of precise and reliable methodologies for safety intervention across diverse tasks. To better understand and control LLM safety, we propose the Expected Safety Impact (ESI) framework for quantifying how different parameters affect LLM safety. Based on ESI, we reveal distinct safety-critical patterns across different LLM architectures: In dense LLMs, many safety-critical parameters are located in value matrices (V) and MLPs in middle layers, whereas in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, they shift to the late-layer MLPs. Leveraging ESI, we further introduce two targeted intervention paradigms for safety enhancement and preservation, i.e., Safety Enhancement Tuning (SET) and Safety Preserving Adaptation (SPA). SET can align unsafe LLMs by updating only a few safety-critical parameters, effectively enhancing safety while preserving original performance. SPA safeguards well-aligned LLMs during capability-oriented intervention (e.g., instruction tuning) by preventing disruption of safety-critical weights, allowing the LLM to acquire new abilities and maintain safety capabilities. Extensive evaluations on different LLMs demonstrate that SET can reduce the attack success rates of unaligned LLMs by over 50% with only a 100-iteration update on 1% of model weights. SPA can limit the safety degradation of aligned LLMs within 1% after a 1,000-iteration instruction fine-tuning on different tasks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ZJU-LLM-Safety/SafeWeights-ACL.

</details>


### [169] [Securing Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Taxonomy of Attacks, Defenses, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08304)
*Yuming Xu,Mingtao Zhang,Zhuohan Ge,Haoyang Li,Nicole Hu,Jason Chen Zhang,Qing Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出检索增强生成（RAG）的安全问题核心在于外部知识访问管道的安全性，通过建立操作边界区分LLM固有缺陷与RAG引入/放大的威胁，并系统化分析攻击、防御和评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM固有风险与RAG特有风险混为一谈，缺乏对RAG引入的安全威胁的系统性分析。论文旨在明确RAG安全问题的本质在于外部知识访问管道的安全性，并建立清晰的威胁分类框架。

Method: 建立操作边界分离LLM固有缺陷与RAG引入/放大的威胁；将RAG工作流程抽象为六个阶段；围绕三个信任边界和四个主要安全面组织文献：预检索知识污染、检索时访问操纵、下游上下文利用和知识泄露。

Result: 系统回顾了对应攻击、防御、修复机制和评估基准，揭示当前防御措施大多是被动和碎片化的，缺乏系统性保护。

Conclusion: 指出当前防御的局限性，强调未来需要在整个知识访问生命周期中实现分层、边界感知的保护，填补现有安全框架的空白。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) significantly enhances large language models (LLMs) but introduces novel security risks through external knowledge access. While existing studies cover various RAG vulnerabilities, they often conflate inherent LLM risks with those specifically introduced by RAG. In this paper, we propose that secure RAG is fundamentally about the security of the external knowledge-access pipeline. We establish an operational boundary to separate inherent LLM flaws from RAG-introduced or RAG-amplified threats. Guided by this perspective, we abstract the RAG workflow into six stages and organize the literature around three trust boundaries and four primary security surfaces, including pre-retrieval knowledge corruption, retrieval-time access manipulation, downstream context exploitation, and knowledge exfiltration. By systematically reviewing the corresponding attacks, defenses, remediation mechanisms, and evaluation benchmarks, we reveal that current defenses remain largely reactive and fragmented. Finally, we discuss these gaps and highlight future directions toward layered, boundary-aware protection across the entire knowledge-access lifecycle.

</details>


### [170] [PIArena: A Platform for Prompt Injection Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08499)
*Runpeng Geng,Chenlong Yin,Yanting Wang,Ying Chen,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PIArena：一个统一的提示注入评估平台，用于系统评估攻击和防御方法，揭示现有防御措施的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入攻击对现实应用构成严重安全威胁，但社区缺乏统一的评估平台，导致难以可靠比较防御方法、理解其真实鲁棒性以及评估跨任务泛化能力

Method: 提出PIArena统一可扩展平台，支持集成最先进的攻击和防御方法，并设计基于防御反馈的自适应优化策略的动态攻击方法

Result: 通过PIArena的全面评估，揭示了最先进防御方法的关键局限性：跨任务泛化能力有限、对自适应攻击易受攻击、当注入任务与目标任务对齐时存在根本性挑战

Conclusion: PIArena填补了提示注入评估的关键空白，为系统评估攻击和防御提供了统一平台，揭示了现有防御方法的严重局限性，强调了开发更鲁棒防御的必要性

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks pose serious security risks across a wide range of real-world applications. While receiving increasing attention, the community faces a critical gap: the lack of a unified platform for prompt injection evaluation. This makes it challenging to reliably compare defenses, understand their true robustness under diverse attacks, or assess how well they generalize across tasks and benchmarks. For instance, many defenses initially reported as effective were later found to exhibit limited robustness on diverse datasets and attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce PIArena, a unified and extensible platform for prompt injection evaluation that enables users to easily integrate state-of-the-art attacks and defenses and evaluate them across a variety of existing and new benchmarks. We also design a dynamic strategy-based attack that adaptively optimizes injected prompts based on defense feedback. Through comprehensive evaluation using PIArena, we uncover critical limitations of state-of-the-art defenses: limited generalizability across tasks, vulnerability to adaptive attacks, and fundamental challenges when an injected task aligns with the target task. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [The Condition-Number Principle for Prototype Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07744)
*Romano Li,Jianfei Cao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出一个几何框架，将聚类算法的目标精度与结构恢复联系起来，定义了聚类条件数来量化聚类问题的内在几何难度，并揭示了鲁棒性与对簇不平衡敏感性之间的基本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类分析通常关注算法性能，但缺乏将目标函数值与实际聚类结构恢复联系起来的理论框架。需要建立算法无关的几何分析，以理解何时低目标函数值能可靠地反映有意义的聚类结构。

Method: 开发几何框架，定义聚类条件数（比较簇内尺度与跨越簇边界所需的最小损失增加）。该框架适用于广泛的容许损失函数类别，分析是算法无关的、确定性的、非渐近的。

Result: 当聚类条件数较小时，任何具有小次优性间隙的解相对于基准划分的误分类误差也小。揭示了鲁棒性与对簇不平衡敏感性之间的权衡，导致不同目标函数下精确恢复的急剧相变。误差集中在簇边界附近，在加强的局部边界条件下，足够深的簇核心能被精确恢复。

Conclusion: 该几何框架提供了将低目标函数值解释为有意义的聚类结构的可靠证据的原则，分离了算法精度与问题实例内在几何难度的作用，为聚类分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a geometric framework that links objective accuracy to structural recovery in prototype-based clustering. The analysis is algorithm-agnostic and applies to a broad class of admissible loss functions. We define a clustering condition number that compares within-cluster scale to the minimum loss increase required to move a point across a cluster boundary. When this quantity is small, any solution with a small suboptimality gap must also have a small misclassification error relative to a benchmark partition. The framework also clarifies a fundamental trade-off between robustness and sensitivity to cluster imbalance, leading to sharp phase transitions for exact recovery under different objectives. The guarantees are deterministic and non-asymptotic, and they separate the role of algorithmic accuracy from the intrinsic geometric difficulty of the instance. We further show that errors concentrate near cluster boundaries and that sufficiently deep cluster cores are recovered exactly under strengthened local margins. Together, these results provide a geometric principle for interpreting low objective values as reliable evidence of meaningful clustering structure.

</details>


### [172] [Order-Optimal Sequential 1-Bit Mean Estimation in General Tail Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07796)
*Ivan Lau,Jonathan Scarlett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出基于随机阈值查询的自适应均值估计器，在1比特通信约束下实现最优样本复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究严格1比特通信约束下的均值估计问题，旨在设计自适应估计器以克服非自适应方法的局限性

Method: 基于随机阈值查询的自适应估计器，通过顺序选择阈值并使用1比特响应（样本是否超过阈值）来估计均值

Result: 对于k≠2的情况，样本复杂度达到未量化极小值下界加定位成本；对于k=2的情况，存在额外的对数惩罚并被证明是1比特量化的基本限制

Conclusion: 自适应方法显著优于非自适应方法，存在显著的适应性差距，并提出了处理未知采样预算和尺度参数的算法变体

Abstract: In this paper, we study the problem of mean estimation under strict 1-bit communication constraints. We propose a novel adaptive mean estimator based solely on randomized threshold queries, where each 1-bit outcome indicates whether a given sample exceeds a sequentially chosen threshold. Our estimator is $(ε, δ)$-PAC for any distribution with a bounded mean $μ\in [-λ, λ]$ and a bounded $k$-th central moment $\mathbb{E}[|X-μ|^k] \le σ^k$ for any fixed $k > 1$. Crucially, our sample complexity is order-optimal in all such tail regimes, i.e., for every such $k$ value. For $k \neq 2$, our estimator's sample complexity matches the unquantized minimax lower bounds plus an unavoidable $O(\log(λ/σ))$ localization cost. For the finite-variance case ($k=2$), our estimator's sample complexity has an extra multiplicative $O(\log(σ/ε))$ penalty, and we establish a novel information-theoretic lower bound showing that this penalty is a fundamental limit of 1-bit quantization. We also establish a significant adaptivity gap: for both threshold queries and more general interval queries, the sample complexity of any non-adaptive estimator must scale linearly with the search space parameter $λ/σ$, rendering it vastly less sample efficient than our adaptive approach. Finally, we present algorithmic variants that (i) handle an unknown sampling budget, (ii) adapt to an unknown scale parameter~$σ$ given (possibly loose) bounds, and (iii) require only two stages of adaptivity at the expense of more complicated general 1-bit queries.

</details>


### [173] [NS-RGS: Newton-Schulz based Riemannian gradient method for orthogonal group synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07372)
*Haiyang Peng,Deren Han,Xin Chen,Meng Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出NS-RGS方法用于正交群同步，用牛顿-舒尔茨迭代替代SVD/QR分解，显著降低计算成本，在GPU/TPU上实现近2倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正交群同步方法依赖每轮迭代的精确SVD或QR分解，计算昂贵且成为大规模问题的瓶颈，需要更高效的算法

Method: 提出牛顿-舒尔茨黎曼梯度方案(NS-RGS)，用牛顿-舒尔茨迭代替代SVD/QR步骤，利用高效矩阵乘法，结合谱初始化，采用改进的留一分析处理统计依赖

Result: NS-RGS在谱初始化下线性收敛到接近最优统计噪声水平的目标解，在合成数据和实际全局对齐任务中达到与广义幂方法相当的精度，实现近2倍加速

Conclusion: NS-RGS为大规模正交群同步问题提供了高效解决方案，显著降低计算成本，与现代硬件架构完美契合，在保持精度的同时大幅提升计算效率

Abstract: Group synchronization is a fundamental task involving the recovery of group elements from pairwise measurements. For orthogonal group synchronization, the most common approach reformulates the problem as a constrained nonconvex optimization and solves it using projection-based methods, such as the generalized power method. However, these methods rely on exact SVD or QR decompositions in each iteration, which are computationally expensive and become a bottleneck for large-scale problems. In this paper, we propose a Newton-Schulz-based Riemannian Gradient Scheme (NS-RGS) for orthogonal group synchronization that significantly reduces computational cost by replacing the SVD or QR step with the Newton-Schulz iteration. This approach leverages efficient matrix multiplications and aligns perfectly with modern GPU/TPU architectures. By employing a refined leave-one-out analysis, we overcome the challenge arising from statistical dependencies, and establish that NS-RGS with spectral initialization achieves linear convergence to the target solution up to near-optimal statistical noise levels. Experiments on synthetic data and real-world global alignment tasks demonstrate that NS-RGS attains accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art methods such as the generalized power method, while achieving nearly a 2$\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [174] [Variational Approximated Restricted Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Spatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07635)
*Debjoy Thakur*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出VREML方法解决空间数据高斯ICAR模型的可扩展推断问题，通过变分近似替代计算昂贵的REML方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统REML方法需要重复计算大型稀疏精度矩阵的逆和分解，计算成本高昂，限制了空间数据模型的可扩展性

Method: 提出变分限制最大似然(VREML)框架，使用高斯变分分布近似难处理的边际似然，构建证据下界(ELBO)，开发坐标上升算法联合估计空间随机效应和方差分量

Result: 理论上证明了ELBO的单调收敛性，数学上展示了在高斯ICAR设置下变分族是精确的，消除了后验层面的近似误差；实证表明VREML优于MLE和INLA方法

Conclusion: VREML为空间数据高斯ICAR模型提供了一种计算高效、可扩展的推断框架，在保持理论精确性的同时显著降低了计算复杂度

Abstract: This research considers a scalable inference for spatial data modeled through Gaussian intrinsic conditional autoregressive (ICAR) structures. The classical estimation method, restricted maximum likelihood (REML), requires repeated inversion and factorization of large, sparse precision matrices, which makes this computation costly. To sort this problem out, we propose a variational restricted maximum likelihood (VREML) framework that approximates the intractable marginal likelihood using a Gaussian variational distribution. By constructing an evidence lower bound (ELBO) on the restricted likelihood, we derive a computationally efficient coordinate-ascent algorithm for jointly estimating the spatial random effects and variance components. In this article, we theoretically establish the monotone convergence of ELBO and mathematically exhibit that the variational family is exact under Gaussian ICAR settings, which is an indication of nullifying approximation error at the posterior level. We empirically establish the supremacy of our VREML over MLE and INLA.

</details>


### [175] [On the Unique Recovery of Transport Maps and Vector Fields from Finite Measure-Valued Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07671)
*Jonah Botvinick-Greenhouse,Yunan Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文建立了从有限测度值数据中唯一恢复向量场和传输映射的理论保证，为生成模型、数据驱动动力系统和PDE反问题提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何从有限的密度数据中唯一确定微分同胚（传输映射）和向量场，这对于生成模型、数据驱动动力系统和PDE反问题的理论分析具有重要意义。

Method: 利用Whitney和Takens嵌入定理，分析微分同胚通过前推作用在有限多个密度上的可识别性条件，并引入基于概率测度空间中前推密度差异的新度量。

Result: 建立了微分同胚可以从有限多个前推密度数据中唯一确定的充分条件，并证明了向量场可以从有限多个加权散度观测中唯一识别的类似结果，所需密度数量仅依赖于问题的内在维度。

Conclusion: 该研究为从有限数据中唯一识别传输映射和向量场提供了理论基础，扩展了Perron-Frobenius和Koopman算子的应用，并为连续性、平流、Fokker-Planck和平流-扩散-反应方程的PDE反问题适定性提供了新保证。

Abstract: We establish guarantees for the unique recovery of vector fields and transport maps from finite measure-valued data, yielding new insights into generative models, data-driven dynamical systems, and PDE inverse problems. In particular, we provide general conditions under which a diffeomorphism can be uniquely identified from its pushforward action on finitely many densities, i.e., when the data $\{(ρ_j,f_\#ρ_j)\}_{j=1}^m$ uniquely determines $f$. As a corollary, we introduce a new metric which compares diffeomorphisms by measuring the discrepancy between finitely many pushforward densities in the space of probability measures. We also prove analogous results in an infinitesimal setting, where derivatives of the densities along a smooth vector field are observed, i.e., when $\{(ρ_j,\text{div} (ρ_j v))\}_{j=1}^m$ uniquely determines $v$. Our analysis makes use of the Whitney and Takens embedding theorems, which provide estimates on the required number of densities $m$, depending only on the intrinsic dimension of the problem. We additionally interpret our results through the lens of Perron--Frobenius and Koopman operators and demonstrate how our techniques lead to new guarantees for the well-posedness of certain PDE inverse problems related to continuity, advection, Fokker--Planck, and advection-diffusion-reaction equations. Finally, we present illustrative numerical experiments demonstrating the unique identification of transport maps from finitely many pushforward densities, and of vector fields from finitely many weighted divergence observations.

</details>


### [176] [Sparse $ε$ insensitive zone bounded asymmetric elastic net support vector machines for pattern classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07748)
*Haiyan Du,Hu Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出ε-BAEN-SVM模型，结合弹性网损失与鲁棒损失框架，构建稀疏ε不敏感有界非对称弹性网损失，解决传统SVM对噪声敏感和缺乏稀疏性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有支持向量机模型对噪声敏感且缺乏稀疏性，限制了其性能。需要开发同时具备稀疏性和鲁棒性的SVM变体。

Method: 结合弹性网损失与鲁棒损失框架构建稀疏ε不敏感有界非对称弹性网损失，集成到SVM中形成ε-BAEN-SVM。采用基于剪裁双坐标下降的半二次算法解决非凸优化问题，通过ε参数将问题转化为一系列加权子问题。

Result: ε-BAEN-SVM在模拟和真实数据集上优于传统和现有鲁棒SVM模型。在稀疏性和鲁棒性之间取得良好平衡，在高斯核下获得更好的准确性和噪声不敏感性，统计测试证实其优越性。

Conclusion: ε-BAEN-SVM同时具备稀疏性和鲁棒性，在噪声环境中表现优异，具有实际应用价值。理论证明样本在ε不敏感带内不是支持向量，影响函数有界保证了鲁棒性。

Abstract: Existing support vector machines(SVM) models are sensitive to noise and lack sparsity, which limits their performance. To address these issues, we combine the elastic net loss with a robust loss framework to construct a sparse $\varepsilon$-insensitive bounded asymmetric elastic net loss, and integrate it with SVM to build $\varepsilon$ Insensitive Zone Bounded Asymmetric Elastic Net Loss-based SVM($\varepsilon$-BAEN-SVM). $\varepsilon$-BAEN-SVM is both sparse and robust. Sparsity is proven by showing that samples inside the $\varepsilon$-insensitive band are not support vectors. Robustness is theoretically guaranteed because the influence function is bounded. To solve the non-convex optimization problem, we design a half-quadratic algorithm based on clipping dual coordinate descent. It transforms the problem into a series of weighted subproblems, improving computational efficiency via the $\varepsilon$ parameter. Experiments on simulated and real datasets show that $\varepsilon$-BAEN-SVM outperforms traditional and existing robust SVMs. It balances sparsity and robustness well in noisy environments. Statistical tests confirm its superiority. Under the Gaussian kernel, it achieves better accuracy and noise insensitivity, validating its effectiveness and practical value.

</details>


### [177] [Intensity Dot Product Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07810)
*Giulio Valentino Dalla Riva,Matteo Dalla Riva*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出Intensity Dot Product Graphs (IDPGs)模型，将RDPG的固定潜在位置替换为泊松点过程，允许随机节点数量，保持点积亲和性，建立连续潜在结构与有限观测图之间的连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在位置随机图模型通常将节点集固定，而图函数和随机测度构造虽然允许更多随机性但几何可解释性较弱。需要一种既能保持RDPG几何解释性，又能引入节点随机性的模型。

Method: 引入Intensity Dot Product Graphs (IDPGs)，用欧几里得潜在空间上的泊松点过程替代RDPG的固定潜在位置集合。定义热图和期望算子作为概率矩阵的连续类比，建立谱一致性理论连接邻接矩阵奇异值与算子谱。

Result: 建立了IDPG模型框架，证明了谱一致性结果，比较了与图函数和双图函数表示的关系，展示了经典RDPG如何在集中极限下出现。由于模型由演化强度参数化，通过偏微分方程自然地扩展到时间序列。

Conclusion: IDPGs提供了一种结合RDPG几何可解释性和节点随机性的统一框架，通过强度函数连接连续潜在结构与有限观测图，为时间扩展提供了自然途径。

Abstract: Latent-position random graph models usually treat the node set as fixed once the sample size is chosen, while graphon-based and random-measure constructions allow more randomness at the cost of weaker geometric interpretability. We introduce \emph{Intensity Dot Product Graphs} (IDPGs), which extend Random Dot Product Graphs by replacing a fixed collection of latent positions with a Poisson point process on a Euclidean latent space. This yields a model with random node populations, RDPG-style dot-product affinities, and a population-level intensity that links continuous latent structure to finite observed graphs. We define the heat map and the desire operator as continuous analogues of the probability matrix, prove a spectral consistency result connecting adjacency singular values to the operator spectrum, compare the construction with graphon and digraphon representations, and show how classical RDPGs arise in a concentrated limit. Because the model is parameterized by an evolving intensity, temporal extensions through partial differential equations arise naturally.

</details>


### [178] [Differentially Private Language Generation and Identification in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08504)
*Anay Mehrotra,Grigoris Velegkas,Xifan Yu,Felix Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在差分隐私约束下研究语言极限生成与识别，发现生成任务中隐私无定性成本但有定量成本，而识别任务中隐私会创造基本障碍，并在对抗性与随机性设置间存在分离


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在差分隐私约束下的语言极限生成与识别问题，探索隐私保护对语言学习任务的影响，特别是比较生成与识别任务在隐私约束下的不同表现

Method: 采用差分隐私框架，研究两种任务：1) 语言极限生成：设计ε-差分隐私算法处理可数语言集合；2) 语言极限识别：分析对抗性和随机性两种设置下的隐私约束影响，通过理论证明建立隐私边界

Result: 1) 生成任务：隐私无定性成本但存在定量成本，可数集合的隐私生成可行，但有限集合需要Ω(k/ε)样本而非非隐私的1个样本；2) 识别任务：隐私创造基本障碍，无法识别具有无限交集和有限集合差的两语言集合，但在随机性设置下隐私识别可行当且仅当在对抗性模型中可识别

Conclusion: 隐私约束在语言生成与识别任务中产生不同影响：生成任务中隐私仅增加定量成本，而识别任务中隐私创造基本障碍；识别任务在对抗性与随机性设置间存在由隐私诱导的分离，揭示了隐私保护下语言学习任务的新维度差异

Abstract: We initiate the study of language generation in the limit, a model recently introduced by Kleinberg and Mullainathan [KM24], under the constraint of differential privacy. We consider the continual release model, where a generator must eventually output a stream of valid strings while protecting the privacy of the entire input sequence. Our first main result is that for countable collections of languages, privacy comes at no qualitative cost: we provide an $\varepsilon$-differentially-private algorithm that generates in the limit from any countable collection. This stands in contrast to many learning settings where privacy renders learnability impossible. However, privacy does impose a quantitative cost: there are finite collections of size $k$ for which uniform private generation requires $Ω(k/\varepsilon)$ samples, whereas just one sample suffices non-privately.
  We then turn to the harder problem of language identification in the limit. Here, we show that privacy creates fundamental barriers. We prove that no $\varepsilon$-DP algorithm can identify a collection containing two languages with an infinite intersection and a finite set difference, a condition far stronger than the classical non-private characterization of identification. Next, we turn to the stochastic setting where the sample strings are sampled i.i.d. from a distribution (instead of being generated by an adversary). Here, we show that private identification is possible if and only if the collection is identifiable in the adversarial model. Together, our results establish new dimensions along which generation and identification differ and, for identification, a separation between adversarial and stochastic settings induced by privacy constraints.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [Data Reuse and the Long Shadow of Error: Splitting, Subsampling, and Prospectively Managing Inferential Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07580)
*Reid Dale,Jordan Rodu,Maria E. Currie,Mike Baiocchi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究在多个研究者共享同一数据集时，由于数据复用导致的测试依赖性及其误差分布问题，提出通过个体研究者层面的子采样技术来最小化协调需求下的依赖性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多个研究者分析同一数据集时，数据复用会导致测试程序间的依赖性，影响误差分布。现有管理依赖性测试的方法需要跨研究协调或事后校正，不适用于当前无协调的独立研究者群体在共享数据集上评估假设的实践。

Method: 建立渐近线性检验统计量的渐近联合正态性，将协方差矩阵分解为数据重叠项和检验统计量关联项的乘积。引入期望方差比（EVR）概念，采用均值-方差投资组合理论衡量风险。证明数据分割在确保精确独立性规则中的渐近最优性，并使用集中不等式证明个体研究者可实施的子采样技术能使EVR接近1。

Result: 子采样技术能够在确保足够统计功效的同时执行多个检验，其有界EVR为O(1/r²)，而数据分割的EVR为O(1/r)，其中r是每个统计量所需的数据比例。

Conclusion: 个体研究者层面的子采样技术能够以最小协调需求有效控制数据复用导致的测试依赖性，提供比数据分割更优的方差控制，适用于无协调的独立研究者群体在共享数据集上的假设检验实践。

Abstract: When multiple investigators analyze a common dataset, the data reuse induces dependence across testing procedures, affecting the distribution of errors. Existing techniques of managing dependent tests require either cross-study coordination or post-hoc correction. These methods do not apply to the current practice of uncoordinated groups of researchers independently evaluating hypotheses on a shared dataset. We investigate the use of subsampling techniques implemented at the level of individual investigators to remedy dependence with minimal coordination.
  To this end, we establish the asymptotic joint normality of test statistics for the class of asymptotically linear test statistics, decomposing the covariance matrix as the product of a data overlap term and a test statistic association term. This decomposition shows that controlling data overlap is sufficient to control dependence, which we formalize through the notion of Expected Variance Ratio.
  This enables the closed form derivation of the variance of the joint rejection region under the global null as a function of pairwise correlations of test statistics. We adopt mean-variance portfolio theory to measure risk, defining the Expected Variance Ratio (EVR) as the ratio of the expected variance of the Type I error count to the independent baseline.
  We show that data splitting is asymptotically optimal among rules that ensure exact independence. We then use concentration inequalities to establish that subsampling techniques implementable by individual investigators can ensure an EVR close to $1$.
  Finally, we show that such subsampling techniques are able to simultaneously perform a number of tests while ensuring sufficient power and that the bounded EVR is $O\left(\frac{1}{r^2}\right)$ compared to data splitting's $O\left(\frac{1}{r}\right)$, where $r$ is the per-statistic fraction of data required.

</details>


### [180] [Consistency of the Bayesian Information Criterion for Model Selection in Exploratory Factor Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07998)
*Hien Duy Nguyen,Kei Hirose*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: BIC在固定维度的探索性因子分析中对模型选择具有强一致性，即使在模型误设情况下，只要满足特定条件，BIC能正确识别伪真实因子阶数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在探索性因子分析中，当模型可能被误设时，贝叶斯信息准则（BIC）是否仍能一致地选择正确的因子阶数。传统方法通常假设模型正确设定，但在实际应用中模型误设是常见情况。

Method: 在固定维度的探索性因子分析框架下，研究BIC在固定有限族紧凑协方差类中的模型选择行为。分析基于伪真实因子阶数的概念，即能提供最佳高斯近似（以Kullback-Leibler散度衡量）的最小候选因子阶数。证明直接在协方差空间进行，避免了正则加载参数化的要求，并能处理由旋转和冗余因子引起的奇异性。

Result: 证明了在满足以下条件时BIC具有强一致性：所有全局最优模型共享共同的伪真实协方差集；总体高斯准则在该集附近具有局部二次边际；BIC复杂度计数在伪真实阶数处具有阶分离性。该结果适用于包含未知均值向量、加载矩阵中精确零限制以及对角或球形误差协方差结构的候选模型。

Conclusion: BIC在探索性因子分析中即使在模型误设情况下也能一致地选择伪真实因子阶数，前提是满足特定的技术条件。该证明框架不依赖于正则参数化，能处理因子分析中的奇异性问题，并可推广到其他满足相同间隙条件的信息准则。

Abstract: We study model selection by the Bayesian information criterion (BIC) in fixed-dimensional exploratory factor analysis over a fixed finite family of compact covariance classes. Our main result shows that the BIC is strongly consistent for the pseudo-true factor order under misspecification, provided that all globally optimal models share a common pseudo-true covariance set, the population Gaussian criterion has a local quadratic margin away from that set, and the BIC complexity counts are order-separating at the pseudo-true order. The candidate models may have an unknown mean vector, exact-zero restrictions in the loading matrix, and either diagonal or spherical error covariance structures, and the selection target is the smallest candidate factor order that yields the best Gaussian approximation, in Kullback--Leibler divergence, to the data-generating covariance structure. The proof works directly in covariance space, so it does not require a regular loading parametrization and accommodates the familiar singularities caused by rotations and redundant factors. Under correct specification, the assumptions reduce to familiar properties of the true covariance matrix. More generally, the same argument applies to other information criteria whose penalties satisfy the same gap conditions, including several BIC-type modifications.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [LLM-Generated Fault Scenarios for Evaluating Perception-Driven Lane Following in Autonomous Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07362)
*Faezeh Pasandideh,Achim Rettberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解耦的离线-在线故障注入框架，使用LLM生成结构化故障场景和LDM合成传感器退化，通过预计算查找表实现边缘设备实时故障感知推理，显著暴露了现有模型在真实环境中的鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备部署自主视觉系统面临资源约束，无法实时执行全面的安全测试。现有验证方法依赖静态数据集或手动故障注入，无法捕捉真实世界部署中遇到的各种环境危害。

Method: 引入解耦的离线-在线故障注入框架：离线阶段使用LLM语义生成结构化故障场景，LDM合成高保真传感器退化，将复杂故障动态蒸馏到预计算查找表中；在线阶段边缘设备通过查找表进行实时故障感知推理，无需本地运行重型AI模型。

Result: 在ResNet18车道跟随模型的460个故障场景上验证：模型在干净数据上R^2约0.85，但生成的故障暴露了显著的鲁棒性退化，RMSE增加高达99%，雾条件下0.10米内定位精度降至31.0%，表明正常数据评估对边缘AI部署不足。

Conclusion: 提出的框架有效暴露了边缘AI系统在真实环境中的鲁棒性缺陷，证明了传统正常数据评估的不足，为边缘设备安全验证提供了实用的离线-在线解耦解决方案。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous vision systems on edge devices faces a critical challenge: resource constraints prevent real-time and predictable execution of comprehensive safety tests. Existing validation methods depend on static datasets or manual fault injection, failing to capture the diverse environmental hazards encountered in real-world deployment. To address this, we introduce a decoupled offline-online fault injection framework. This architecture separates the validation process into two distinct phases: a computationally intensive Offline Phase and a lightweight Online Phase. In the offline phase, we employ Large Language Models (LLMs) to semantically generate structured fault scenarios and Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) to synthesize high-fidelity sensor degradations. These complex fault dynamics are distilled into a pre-computed lookup table, enabling the edge device to perform real-time fault-aware inference without running heavy AI models locally. We extensively validated this framework on a ResNet18 lane-following model across 460 fault scenarios. Results show that while the model achieves a baseline R^2 of approximately 0.85 on clean data, our generated faults expose significant robustness degradation, with RMSE increasing by up to 99% and within-0.10 localization accuracy dropping to as low as 31.0% under fog conditions, demonstrating the inadequacy of normal-data evaluation for real-world edge AI deployment.

</details>


### [182] [Benchmark Shadows: Data Alignment, Parameter Footprints, and Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07363)
*Hongjian Zou,Yidan Wang,Qi Ding,Yixuan Liao,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现基准对齐数据提升窄评估指标但限制广泛表征发展，而覆盖扩展数据促进分布式参数适应和更好泛化，表明基准性能不足以表征模型能力，数据分布对学习动态至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常在基准测试中获得显著提升，但这些提升并未对应到更广泛能力的改善。作者假设这种差异源于数据分布引起的训练机制差异，旨在通过受控数据干预来研究数据分布对模型能力发展的影响。

Method: 设计受控数据干预实验，在固定训练设置下隔离分布效应；引入基于谱分析和秩分析的参数空间诊断方法；在多样开源模型家族（包括多模态模型作为关键案例研究）中进行验证；通过提示重复案例研究检验数据伪影的影响。

Result: 基准对齐数据改善窄评估指标但限制广泛表征发展；覆盖扩展数据导致更分布式参数适应和更好泛化；谱和秩分析揭示不同训练机制的结构特征；这些模式在多样模型家族中普遍存在；提示重复案例表明并非所有数据伪影都会引起机制转变。

Conclusion: 基准性能单独不足以表征模型能力，数据分布在塑造学习动态中起关键作用。研究强调了需要超越基准评估来理解模型能力发展，并为数据分布如何影响模型学习提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Large language models often achieve strong benchmark gains without corresponding improvements in broader capability. We hypothesize that this discrepancy arises from differences in training regimes induced by data distribution. To investigate this, we design controlled data interventions that isolate distributional effects under fixed training settings.
